# Methodology claim audit

**Observation:** These are baseline claims from the pre-edit working copy, not a score of the corrected document. Each row is a sentence, list assertion, table parameter row, or formula. Repeated statements remain separate occurrences. Pure diagrams/analogies are not executable claims; source-index entries are evaluated individually in source-audit.md. Baseline line references refer to SHA-256 `af006d1cee00f3e353e57858c7de12212ff6c2d4bc90d1cc988875546197810a`; current headings remain stable.

**Classification:** IMPLEMENTED includes active prompt requirements; it does not mean executable enforcement. Test scope is stated per row. Retired parameter rows retain historical interpretation without current-effectiveness claims. Required correction describes the applied section rewrite; rows classified STALE/UNSUPPORTED are not represented as current behavior in the revision. Residual conflicts are separately retained in findings.md.
